Empirical results demonstrate that its stochastic version, AmelPredSto, is the best-performing SRL model when combined with actor-critic RL algorithms. The obtained results show substantial improvement in RL algorithms' efficiency by using AmelPredSto in solving the OGN problem.

</details>


### [79] [Navigating the Clutter: Waypoint-Based Bi-Level Planning for Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21138)
*Jiabao Ji,Yongchao Chen,Yang Zhang,Ramana Rao Kompella,Chuchu Fan,Gaowen Liu,Shiyu Chang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合多机器人控制框架，通过引入路径点表示和课程式RLVR训练策略，联合优化任务规划与运动规划，在密集障碍物环境中显著提升了多机器人任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杂乱环境中多机器人控制面临机器人之间碰撞、机器人与障碍物碰撞以及不可达运动等复杂物理约束，需要联合优化高层任务规划和低层运动规划，但两者联合优化因运动轨迹参数化复杂和跨层信用分配模糊而十分困难。

Method: 1) 提出使用路径点作为运动轨迹的简洁表达，实现低层规划的有效参数化；2) 采用基于课程的训练策略，结合改进的RLVR算法，将运动规划器的运动可行性反馈传播到任务规划器，解决信用分配问题。

Result: 在包含密集障碍物、最多9个机器人的BoxNet3D-OBS基准测试中，该方法在任务成功率上持续优于忽略运动的基线方法和VLA基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过联合优化任务与运动规划，该混合框架有效解决了杂乱环境中的多机器人控制问题，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-robot control in cluttered environments is a challenging problem that involves complex physical constraints, including robot-robot collisions, robot-obstacle collisions, and unreachable motions. Successful planning in such settings requires joint optimization over high-level task planning and low-level motion planning, as violations of physical constraints may arise from failures at either level. However, jointly optimizing task and motion planning is difficult due to the complex parameterization of low-level motion trajectories and the ambiguity of credit assignment across the two planning levels. In this paper, we propose a hybrid multi-robot control framework that jointly optimizes task and motion planning. To enable effective parameterization of low-level planning, we introduce waypoints, a simple yet expressive representation for motion trajectories. To address the credit assignment challenge, we adopt a curriculum-based training strategy with a modified RLVR algorithm that propagates motion feasibility feedback from the motion planner to the task planner. Experiments on BoxNet3D-OBS, a challenging multi-robot benchmark with dense obstacles and up to nine robots, show that our approach consistently improves task success over motion-agnostic and VLA-based baselines. Our code is available at https://github.com/UCSB-NLP-Chang/navigate-cluster

</details>


### [80] [Full-Body Dynamic Safety for Robot Manipulators: 3D Poisson Safety Functions for CBF-Based Safety Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21189)
*Meg Wilkinson,Gilbert Bahati,Ryan M. Bena,Emily Fourney,Joel W. Burdick,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于3D泊松安全函数的机械臂全身避碰框架，通过构造全局平滑的控制障碍函数，在保证理论安全性的同时降低了多约束计算的复杂度，并在七自由度机械臂上进行了动态环境验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械臂的全身避碰需要在高维构型空间中施加大量安全约束，基于控制障碍函数的安全滤波器虽有效，但处理全身安全所需的大量约束会带来理论和计算上的挑战。

Method: 利用3D泊松安全函数，对环境占据数据进行采样，通过庞特里亚金差分根据采样分辨率收缩自由空间，在缓冲区域上求解泊松方程合成全局光滑的CBF，并通过多约束二次规划实现实时安全滤波。

Result: 证明了在缓冲区域内保持采样点安全即可保证整个连续机械臂表面的避碰，并在七自由度机械臂动态环境中验证了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提框架通过单安全函数与多约束QP的结合，有效解决了全身避碰中大量约束带来的理论和计算难题，实现了动态环境下的实时安全控制。

Abstract: Collision avoidance for robotic manipulators requires enforcing full-body safety constraints in high-dimensional configuration spaces. Control Barrier Function (CBF) based safety filters have proven effective in enabling safe behaviors, but enforcing the high number of constraints needed for safe manipulation leads to theoretic and computational challenges. This work presents a framework for full-body collision avoidance for manipulators in dynamic environments by leveraging 3D Poisson Safety Functions (PSFs). In particular, given environmental occupancy data, we sample the manipulator surface at a prescribed resolution and shrink free space via a Pontryagin difference according to this resolution. On this buffered domain, we synthesize a globally smooth CBF by solving Poisson's equation, yielding a single safety function for the entire environment. This safety function, evaluated at each sampled point, yields task-space CBF constraints enforced by a real-time safety filter via a multi-constraint quadratic program. We prove that keeping the sample points safe in the buffered region guarantees collision avoidance for the entire continuous robot surface. The framework is validated on a 7-degree-of-freedom manipulator in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [81] [How VLAs (Really) Work In Open-World Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21192)
*Amir Rasouli,Yangzheng Wu,Zhiyuan Li,Rui Heng Yang,Xuan Zhao,Charles Eret,Sajjad Pakdamansavoji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文指出现有的视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）在BEHAVIOR1K等基准上的评估仅关注最终状态成功率，忽略了安全性和操作过程。作者进行了深入分析，发现这种评估夸大了性能，并提出了包含安全违规检测的新评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型在长时域任务中的评估仅基于最终状态（与过程无关的标准），忽略了操作过程中的安全性和鲁棒性，这掩盖了实际部署中的关键挑战，可能导致性能虚高。

Method: 对B1K Challenge上的SOTA模型进行深入分析，从可重复性（复现性）、性能一致性、操作安全性、任务感知能力以及任务未完成的关键因素五个维度评估策略。提出能够捕捉安全违规行为的新评估协议。

Result: 现有评估协议会导致性能被高估；SOTA模型在安全性、可复现性和一致性方面存在显著不足；安全违规是导致任务失败的关键因素之一。

Conclusion: 需要引入考虑安全性的评估协议来更真实地衡量VLA策略在复杂交互场景中的性能；现有VLA存在局限性，未来应关注安全性和过程鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) have been extensively used in robotics applications, achieving great success in various manipulation problems. More recently, VLAs have been used in long-horizon tasks and evaluated on benchmarks, such as BEHAVIOR1K (B1K), for solving complex household chores. The common metric for measuring progress in such benchmarks is success rate or partial score based on satisfaction of progress-agnostic criteria, meaning only the final states of the objects are considered, regardless of the events that lead to such states. In this paper, we argue that using such evaluation protocols say little about safety aspects of operation and can potentially exaggerate reported performance, undermining core challenges for future real-world deployment. To this end, we conduct a thorough analysis of state-of-the-art models on the B1K Challenge and evaluate policies in terms of robustness via reproducibility and consistency of performance, safety aspects of policies operations, task awareness, and key elements leading to the incompletion of tasks. We then propose evaluation protocols to capture safety violations to better measure the true performance of the policies in more complex and interactive scenarios. At the end, we discuss the limitations of the existing VLAs and motivate future research.

</details>


### [82] [CorridorVLA: Explicit Spatial Constraints for Generative Action Heads via Sparse Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21241)
*Dachong Li,ZhuangZhuang Chen,Jin Zhang,Jianqiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CorridorVLA通过预测稀疏空间锚点（增量物理变化）构建显式容差区域（走廊），引导流匹配动作头生成动作轨迹，在LIBERO-Plus基准上相比基线提升3.4%-12.4%的成功率，GR00T-Corr变体达到83.21%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型常通过隐式潜特征注入空间引导，缺乏显式且可解释的物理约束来指导动作生成，导致在复杂任务中表现受限。

Method: 提出CorridorVLA：1）预测稀疏空间锚点作为增量物理变化（Δ-位置）；2）在训练目标中构建显式容差区域（走廊）；3）使用流匹配动作头生成轨迹，对超出走廊的轨迹施加修正梯度，同时容忍微小偏差。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus基准上，SmolVLA和GR00T变体均获得一致提升（3.4%-12.4%），GR00T-Corr达到83.21%成功率。

Conclusion: 动作对齐的物理线索能够为生成式动作策略提供直接且可解释的约束，补充视觉或隐式形式编码的空间引导。

Abstract: Vision--Language--Action (VLA) models often use intermediate representations to connect multimodal inputs with continuous control, yet spatial guidance is often injected implicitly through latent features. We propose $CorridorVLA$, which predicts sparse spatial anchors as incremental physical changes (e.g., $Δ$-positions) and uses them to impose an explicit tolerance region in the training objective for action generation. The anchors define a corridor that guides a flow-matching action head: trajectories whose implied spatial evolution falls outside it receive corrective gradients, while minor deviations from contacts and execution noise are permitted. On the more challenging LIBERO-Plus benchmark, CorridorVLA yields consistent gains across both SmolVLA and GR00T, improving success rate by $3.4\%$--$12.4\%$ over the corresponding baselines; notably, our GR00T-Corr variant reaches a success rate of $83.21\%$. These results indicate that action-aligned physical cues can provide direct and interpretable constraints for generative action policies, complementing spatial guidance encoded in visual or latent forms. Code is available at https://github.com/corridorVLA.

</details>


### [83] [Reasoning About Traversability: Language-Guided Off-Road 3D Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21249)
*Byounggun Park,Soonmin Hwang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种语言精炼框架，将离路驾驶数据中的弱对齐标注重构为动作对齐对，结合偏好优化策略提升VLMs在非结构化环境中的轨迹规划能力，在ORAD-3D基准上将轨迹误差从1.01m降至0.97m，并显著改善了地形通过性和高程一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离路数据集的语言标注与车辆动作和地形几何弱对齐，导致VLMs无法有效进行地形感知的轨迹规划。

Method: 1) 语言精炼框架，将标注重构为动作对齐对；2) 偏好优化策略，构建几何感知硬负样本并惩罚与局部高程不一致的轨迹；3) 提出离路专用评估指标（通过性合规、高程一致性）。

Result: 在ORAD-3D基准上：平均轨迹误差从1.01m降至0.97m，通过性合规从0.621提升至0.644，高程不一致性从0.428降至0.322。

Conclusion: 动作对齐监督和地形感知优化能有效提升VLMs在离路驾驶场景中的鲁棒规划能力。

Abstract: While Vision-Language Models (VLMs) enable high-level semantic reasoning for end-to-end autonomous driving, particularly in unstructured environments, existing off-road datasets suffer from language annotations that are weakly aligned with vehicle actions and terrain geometry. To address this misalignment, we propose a language refinement framework that restructures annotations into action-aligned pairs, enabling a VLM to generate refined scene descriptions and 3D future trajectories directly from a single image. To further encourage terrain-aware planning, we introduce a preference optimization strategy that constructs geometry-aware hard negatives and explicitly penalizes trajectories inconsistent with local elevation profiles. Furthermore, we propose off-road-specific metrics to quantify traversability compliance and elevation consistency, addressing the limitations of conventional on-road evaluation. Experiments on the ORAD-3D benchmark demonstrate that our approach reduces average trajectory error from 1.01m to 0.97m, improves traversability compliance from 0.621 to 0.644, and decreases elevation inconsistency from 0.428 to 0.322, highlighting the efficacy of action-aligned supervision and terrain-aware optimization for robust off-road driving.

</details>


### [84] [FingerViP: Learning Real-World Dexterous Manipulation with Fingertip Visual Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21331)
*Zhen Zhang,Weinan Wang,Hejia Sun,Qingpeng Ding,Xiangyu Chu,Guoxin Fang,K. W. Samuel Au*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FingerViP系统，通过在灵巧手每个指尖嵌入微型相机，结合扩散策略的全身视觉运动策略，利用多视角指尖视觉感知解决单腕部视角遮挡问题，在多项复杂任务上达到80.8%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前灵巧操作普遍依赖单腕部视角，存在严重的遮挡问题，限制了需要多视角感知的复杂任务性能。

Method: 设计指尖增强视觉模块（嵌入微型相机），安装于多指手的每个手指上；开发基于扩散模型的全身视觉运动策略，以第三视角相机和多视角指尖视觉为条件，从人类演示中学习；每个指尖视觉特征增强对应相机位姿编码和指关节电流编码，以改善视角-本体感知对齐和接触感知。

Result: 在按钮按压、物体取回、遮挡取回、长时序柜门开启与物体取回等多项真实世界挑战性任务上实现80.8%的总体成功率。

Conclusion: 多视角指尖视觉能有效提升灵巧操作的感知鲁棒性和任务适应性，硬件设计与代码将完全开源。

Abstract: The current practice of dexterous manipulation generally relies on a single wrist-mounted view, which is often occluded and limits performance on tasks requiring multi-view perception. In this work, we present FingerViP, a learning system that utilizes a visuomotor policy with fingertip visual perception for dexterous manipulation. Specifically, we design a vision-enhanced fingertip module with an embedded miniature camera and install the modules on each finger of a multi-fingered hand. The fingertip cameras substantially improve visual perception by providing comprehensive, multi-view feedback of both the hand and its surrounding environment. Building on the integrated fingertip modules, we develop a diffusion-based whole-body visuomotor policy conditioned on a third-view camera and multi-view fingertip vision, which effectively learns complex manipulation skills directly from human demonstrations. To improve view-proprioception alignment and contact awareness, each fingertip visual feature is augmented with its corresponding camera pose encoding and per-finger joint-current encoding. We validate the effectiveness of the multi-view fingertip vision and demonstrate the robustness and adaptability of FingerViP on various challenging real-world tasks, including pressing buttons inside a confined box, retrieving sticks from an unstable support, retrieving objects behind an occluding curtain, and performing long-horizon cabinet opening and object retrieval, achieving an overall success rate of 80.8%. All hardware designs and code will be fully open-sourced.

</details>


### [85] [PREVENT-JACK: Context Steering for Swarms of Long Heavy Articulated Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21337)
*Adrian Baruck,Michael Dubé,Christoph Steup,Sanaz Mostaghim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Prevent-Jack方法，将稀疏覆盖上下文引导框架应用于重型铰接车辆（HAVs）群集，融合六种局部行为以防止折叠和碰撞，通过15000次仿真评估了最多十节拖车的车辆群性能，发现死锁和活锁在更大群集和密集场景中更频繁发生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统群集机器人研究多采用点状质点机器人模型，而真实世界中存在大量具有运动学约束、细长且铰接的重型车辆（HAVs），其协同控制面临独特挑战。本文旨在将群集机器人研究从理想化点模型扩展到更具现实意义的HAVs，以满足实际应用需求。

Method: 提出Prevent-Jack方法，引入稀疏覆盖上下文引导框架，融合六种局部行为（包括对死锁预防关键的Evade Attraction行为），通过参数研究分析各行为重要性，并利用15000次仿真对最多十节拖车的车辆群进行性能评估。

Result: 死锁和活锁在更大群集和密集场景中更频繁发生，峰值平均影响27%/31%的车辆。大群集表现出更多等待行为，小群集则表现出更多规避行为。

Conclusion: Prevent-Jack方法能在避免折叠和碰撞的同时提供保障，但代价是可能产生死锁和活锁。Evade Attraction行为对死锁预防至关重要。

Abstract: In this paper, we aim to extend the traditional point-mass-like robot representation in swarm robotics and instead study a swarm of long Heavy Articulated Vehicles (HAVs). HAVs are kinematically constrained, elongated, and articulated, introducing unique challenges. Local, decentralized coordination of these vehicles is motivated by many real-world applications. Our approach, Prevent-Jack, introduces the sparsely covered context steering framework in robotics. It fuses six local behaviors, providing guarantees against jackknifing and collisions at the cost of potential dead- and livelocks, tested for vehicles with up to ten trailers. We highlight the importance of the Evade Attraction behavior for deadlock prevention using a parameter study, and use 15,000 simulations to evaluate the swarm performance. Our extensive experiments and the results show that both the dead- and livelocks occur more frequently in larger swarms and denser scenarios, affecting a peak average of 27%/31% of vehicles. We observe that larger swarms exhibit increased waiting, while smaller swarms show increased evasion.

</details>


### [86] [Learn Weightlessness: Imitate Non-Self-Stabilizing Motions on Humanoid Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21351)
*Yucheng Xin,Jiacheng Bao,Haoran Yang,Wenqiang Que,Dong Wang,Junbo Tan,Xueqian Wang,Bin Zhao,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受生物启发的“失重机制”（Weightlessness Mechanism, WM），通过自动标注数据集并动态决定放松哪些关节及程度，使类人机器人能够在执行非自稳定（NSS）动作时有效与环境交互，在多样化环境配置下实现强泛化，且无需任务特定调优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类人机器人全身控制方法在环境依赖型运动方面研究有限，通常强制刚性轨迹跟踪而忽视与环境的物理交互，限制了机器人在复杂接触场景中的适应能力。

Method: 设计了一种失重状态自动标注策略用于数据集构建；提出失重机制（WM），动态决定哪些关节应放松及放松程度，从而在执行目标动作的同时实现有效的环境交互。

Result: 在3种代表性NSS任务（不同高度椅子坐下、不同倾斜度床躺下、靠墙）上的仿真和Unitree G1机器人实验中，WM方法在仅使用单一动作示范训练且无任务特定调优的情况下，在多样环境配置中展现了强泛化能力和运动稳定性。

Conclusion: 本研究弥合了精确轨迹跟踪与环境自适应交互之间的鸿沟，为接触丰富的类人机器人控制提供了一种生物启发的解决方案。

Abstract: The integration of imitation and reinforcement learning has enabled remarkable advances in humanoid whole-body control, facilitating diverse human-like behaviors. However, research on environment-dependent motions remains limited. Existing methods typically enforce rigid trajectory tracking while neglecting physical interactions with the environment. We observe that humans naturally exploit a "weightless" state during non-self-stabilizing (NSS) motions--selectively relaxing specific joints to allow passive body--environment contact, thereby stabilizing the body and completing the motion. Inspired by this biological mechanism, we design a weightlessness-state auto-labeling strategy for dataset annotation; and we propose the Weightlessness Mechanism (WM), a method that dynamically determines which joints to relax and to what level, together enabling effective environmental interaction while executing target motions. We evaluate our approach on 3 representative NSS tasks: sitting on chairs of varying heights, lying down on beds with different inclinations, and leaning against walls via shoulder or elbow. Extensive experiments in simulation and on the Unitree G1 robot demonstrate that our WM method, trained on single-action demonstrations without any task-specific tuning, achieves strong generalization across diverse environmental configurations while maintaining motion stability. Our work bridges the gap between precise trajectory tracking and adaptive environmental interaction, offering a biologically-inspired solution for contact-rich humanoid control.

</details>


### [87] [RPG: Robust Policy Gating for Smooth Multi-Skill Transitions in Humanoid Fighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21355)
*Yucheng Xin,Jiacheng Bao,Yubo Dong,Xueqian Wang,Bin Zhao,Xuelong Li,Junbo Tan,Dong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RPG混合专家策略框架，通过运动过渡随机化和时间随机化训练统一策略，实现人形机器人多技能平滑稳定过渡，支持长时间动态格斗任务，并在仿真和Unitree G1实物机器人上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形机器人格斗方法依赖多单技能策略切换或通用策略模仿参考动作，技能间初始/终止状态不匹配导致域外扰动，引发不平稳或不稳定行为，亟需一种能实现多技能平滑稳定过渡的框架。

Method: 提出RPG混合专家策略框架，包含运动过渡随机化和时间随机化训练统一策略；设计集成行走/跑步运动与格斗技能的控制管线，支持任意时长可无缝中断或随时切换动作策略的长时间格斗。

Result: 仿真实验证明框架有效性，在Unitree G1人形机器人上的实物部署进一步验证了鲁棒性和实用性。

Conclusion: RPG框架有效解决了人形机器人多技能过渡的不稳定问题，实现了类人长时间动态格斗，具备良好的迁移性和实际部署能力。

Abstract: Humanoid robots have demonstrated impressive motor skills in a wide range of tasks, yet whole-body control for humanlike long-time, dynamic fighting remains particularly challenging due to the stringent requirements on agility and stability. While imitation learning enables robots to execute human-like fighting skills, existing approaches often rely on switching among multiple single-skill policies or employing a general policy to imitate input reference motions. These strategies suffer from instability when transitioning between skills, as the mismatch of initial and terminal states across skills or reference motions introduces out-of-domain disturbances, resulting in unsmooth or unstable behaviors. In this work, we propose RPG, a hybrid expert policy framework, for smooth and stable humanoid multi-skills transition. Our approach incorporates motion transition randomization and temporal randomization to train a unified policy that generates agile fighting actions with stability and smoothness during skill transitions. Furthermore, we design a control pipeline that integrates walking/running locomotion with fighting skills, allowing humanlike long-time combat of arbitrary duration that can be seamlessly interrupted or transit action policies at any time. Extensive experiments in simulation demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework, and real-world deployment on the Unitree G1 humanoid robot further validates its robustness and applicability.

</details>


### [88] [A Deployable Embodied Vision-Language Navigation System with Hierarchical Cognition and Context-Aware Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21363)
*Kuan Xu,Ruimeng Liu,Yizhuo Yang,Denan Liang,Tongxing Jin,Shenghai Yuan,Chen Wang,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可部署的具身视觉语言导航系统，通过将系统解耦为实时感知、记忆集成和推理三个异步模块，并构建认知记忆图结合加权旅行修理工问题（WTRP），在资源受限硬件上实现了高效鲁棒的高层推理与实时导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN方法在强大推理能力与高效部署之间存在根本性权衡，难以同时在真实机器人平台上实现强推理与高效率。

Method: 系统解耦为三个异步模块：实时感知模块、记忆集成模块（增量构建认知记忆图并分解为子图供VLM推理）和推理模块（将探索问题建模为上下文感知的加权旅行修理工问题WTRP，最小化视点加权等待时间）。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人平台上的大量实验表明，该方法在导航成功率和效率上优于现有VLN方法，同时在资源受限硬件上保持了实时性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的解耦架构和认知记忆图方法有效弥合了具身智能与嵌入式部署之间的差距，为VLN在真实机器人平台上的实际应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Bridging the gap between embodied intelligence and embedded deployment remains a key challenge in intelligent robotic systems, where perception, reasoning, and planning must operate under strict constraints on computation, memory, energy, and real-time execution. In vision-language navigation (VLN), existing approaches often face a fundamental trade-off between strong reasoning capabilities and efficient deployment on real-world platforms. In this paper, we present a deployable embodied VLN system that achieves both high efficiency and robust high-level reasoning on real-world robotic platforms. To achieve this, we decouple the system into three asynchronous modules: a real-time perception module for continuous environment sensing, a memory integration module for spatial-semantic aggregation, and a reasoning module for high-level decision making. We incrementally construct a cognitive memory graph to encode scene information, which is further decomposed into subgraphs to enable reasoning with a vision-language model (VLM). To further improve navigation efficiency and accuracy, we also leverage the cognitive memory graph to formulate the exploration problem as a context-aware Weighted Traveling Repairman Problem (WTRP), which minimizes the weighted waiting time of viewpoints. Extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world robotic platforms demonstrate improved navigation success and efficiency over existing VLN approaches, while maintaining real-time performance on resource-constrained hardware.

</details>


### [89] [A Replicable Robotics Awareness Method Using LLM-Enabled Robotics Interaction: Evidence from a Corporate Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21377)
*S. A. Prieto,M. A. Gopee,Y. Ben Arab,B. García de Soto,J. Esteba,P. Olivera Brizzio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一种基于挑战的机器人认知方法，通过LLM驱动的人形机器人活动，在阿布扎比港口集团员工中开展。活动采用团队协作、角色驱动的任务形式，参与者通过语音指令与机器人交互。102份调查结果显示总体反响良好（满意度8.46/10），显著提升了参与者对机器人/AI的兴趣（4.47/5）和人机协作理解（4.45/5），但可靠性和可预测性评分较低，指出了未来改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏将LLM作为结构化手段向非专业用户在真实组织环境中引入机器人技术的实证研究。作者希望探索一种无需先验专业知识即可让普通员工接触具身AI与人机协作的有效方法。

Method: 设计了一个挑战式活动，使用LLM控制框架的人形机器人，在物流启发任务环境中让参与者通过语音指令与机器人交互。活动为团队合作、角色驱动体验。通过为期16天的在线问卷收集102份反馈进行定量评估。

Result: 整体满意度8.46/10；对机器人/AI兴趣提升4.47/5；人机协作理解提升4.45/5；直接交互者认为交互自然4.37/5；交互随活动进行变得更容易4.74/5；可靠性和可预测性评分较低。

Conclusion: 基于挑战的LLM驱动人形机器人交互是一种有前景且可复制的机器人认知方法，适用于工业和运营环境，但需改进系统的可靠性与可预测性。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly being explored as interfaces between humans and robotic systems, yet there remains limited evidence on how such technologies can be used not only for interaction, but also as a structured means of introducing robotics to non-specialist users in real organizational settings. This paper introduces and evaluates a challenge-based method for robotics awareness, implemented through an LLM-enabled humanoid robot activity conducted with employees of AD Ports Group in the United Arab Emirates. In the event, participants engaged with a humanoid robot in a logistics-inspired task environment using voice commands interpreted through an LLM-based control framework. The activity was designed as a team-based, role-driven experience intended to expose participants to embodied AI and human-robot collaboration without requiring prior robotics expertise. To evaluate the approach, a post-event survey remained open for 16 days and collected 102 responses. Results indicate strong overall reception, with high satisfaction (8.46/10), increased interest in robotics and AI (4.47/5), and improved understanding of emerging forms of human-robot collaboration (4.45/5). Participants who interacted directly with the robot also reported natural interaction (4.37/5) and a strong sense that interaction became easier as the activity progressed (4.74/5). At the same time, lower ratings for reliability and predictability point to important technical and design challenges for future iterations. The findings suggest that challenge-based, LLM-enabled humanoid interaction can serve as a promising and replicable method for robotics awareness in industrial and operational environments.

</details>


### [90] [From Noise to Intent: Anchoring Generative VLA Policies with Residual Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21391)
*Yiming Zhong,Yaoyu He,Zemin Yang,Pengfei Tian,Yifan Huang,Qingqiu Huang,Xinge Zhu,Yuexin Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ResVLA提出了一种从“噪声生成”转向“意图精炼”的新范式，通过频谱分析将机器人控制解耦为确定性的低频锚点（全局意图）和随机的高频残差（局部动力学），利用残差扩散桥仅对局部动态进行精炼，从而弥合高层语义理解与低层物理控制之间的跨尺度鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式VLA策略采用“从噪声生成”范式，忽视了认知与行动之间的时空尺度不匹配问题，导致表示效率低下且条件对齐弱。

Method: 利用频谱分析将机器人运动解耦为确定性低频锚点（全局意图）和随机高频残差（局部动力学），以预测的意图为锚点，通过残差扩散桥仅对局部动态进行精炼。

Result: 在仿真实验中取得了具有竞争力的性能，对语言和机器人形态扰动具有强鲁棒性，收敛速度快于标准生成式基线；在真实机器人实验中同样表现强劲。

Conclusion: 将生成范式从“噪声生成”转变为“意图精炼”能有效解决认知与行动间的跨尺度问题，提升表示效率与条件对齐能力。

Abstract: Bridging high-level semantic understanding with low-level physical control remains a persistent challenge in embodied intelligence, stemming from the fundamental spatiotemporal scale mismatch between cognition and action. Existing generative VLA policies typically adopt a "Generation-from-Noise" paradigm, which disregards this disparity, leading to representation inefficiency and weak condition alignment during optimization. In this work, we propose ResVLA, an architecture that shifts the paradigm to "Refinement-from-Intent." Recognizing that robotic motion naturally decomposes into global intent and local dynamics, ResVLA utilizes spectral analysis to decouple control into a deterministic low-frequency anchor and a stochastic high-frequency residual. By anchoring the generative process on the predicted intent, our model focuses strictly on refining local dynamics via a residual diffusion bridge. Extensive simulation experiments show that ResVLA achieves competitive performance, strong robustness to language and robot embodiment perturbations, and faster convergence than standard generative baselines. It also demonstrates strong performance in real-world robot experiments.

</details>


### [91] [Ufil: A Unified Framework for Infrastructure-based Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21471)
*Simon Schäfer,Lucas Hegerath,Marius Molz,Massimo Marcon,Bassam Alrifaee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Ufil，一个面向基础设施定位的统一框架，提供标准化对象模型和可复用多目标跟踪组件，支持异构数据源融合。在CARLA仿真和小规模测试平台上，融合系统实现了车道级横向精度（RMSE约0.3m）和低于100ms的中位端到端延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础设施定位系统采用碎片化、应用特定的堆栈，将感知、跟踪和中间件紧耦合，导致开发困难、复用性差。

Method: 提出Ufil框架，设计标准化对象模型和可复用多目标跟踪组件（预测、检测、关联、状态更新、轨迹管理），并集成ETSI ITS-G5 CAM、激光雷达路侧节点、道路敏感表面层三种异构数据源。框架基于C++/ROS 2开源实现。

Result: 在三车道高速公路场景中（仿真423辆车、测试台355辆车），融合系统在CARLA中横向RMSE为0.31m，在CPM Lab为0.29m，平均绝对方向误差约2.2°；所有模态的中位端到端延迟均低于100ms。

Conclusion: Ufil验证了其尺度无关的执行模型，能够在不修改管线的情况下在仿真器和真实测试平台上运行，为基础设施定位提供了统一的模块化框架，降低了研究门槛。

Abstract: Infrastructure-based localization enhances road safety and traffic management by providing state estimates of road users. Development is hindered by fragmented, application-specific stacks that tightly couple perception, tracking, and middleware. We introduce Ufil, a Unified Framework for Infrastructure-Based Localization with a standardized object model and reusable multi-object tracking components. Ufil offers interfaces and reference implementations for prediction, detection, association, state update, and track management, allowing researchers to improve components without reimplementing the pipeline. Ufil is open-source C++/ROS 2 software with documentation and executable examples. We demonstrate Ufil by integrating three heterogeneous data sources into a single localization pipeline combining (i) vehicle onboard units broadcasting ETSI ITS-G5 Cooperative Awareness Messages, (ii) a lidar-based roadside sensor node, and (iii) an in-road sensitive surface layer. The pipeline runs unchanged in the CARLA simulator and a small-scale CAV testbed, demonstrating Ufil's scale-independent execution model. In a three-lane highway scenario with 423 and 355 vehicles in simulation and testbed, respectively, the fused system achieves lane-level lateral accuracy with mean lateral position RMSEs of 0.31 m in CARLA and 0.29 m in the CPM Lab, and mean absolute orientation errors around 2.2°. Median end-to-end latencies from sensing to fused output remain below 100 ms across all modalities in both environments.

</details>


### [92] [MISTY: High-Throughput Motion Planning via Mixer-based Single-step Drifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21489)
*Yining Xing,Zehong Ke,Yiqian Tu,Zhiyuan Liu,Wenhao Yu,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MISTY是一种基于MLP-Mixer的高通量生成式运动规划器，通过单步推理、32维潜空间编码和潜空间漂移损失，在nuPlan基准上达到SOTA性能（Test14-hard：非反应式80.32，反应式82.21），推理速度超过99 FPS（10.1 ms），相比迭代扩散规划器实现数量级加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的轨迹规划器因迭代神经函数评估导致高推理延迟，难以满足自动驾驶对实时性的要求。

Method: 1) 向量化子图编码器捕获环境上下文；2) VAE将专家轨迹压缩至32维潜流形；3) 超轻量MLP-Mixer解码器消除二次注意力复杂度；4) 引入潜空间漂移损失，将分布演化转移至训练阶段，通过显式吸引-排斥力实现主动超车等新异策略。

Result: 在nuPlan Test14-hard上非反应式得分80.32，反应式得分82.21；推理速度超99 FPS，端到端延迟10.1 ms，相较迭代扩散规划器实现数量级加速。

Conclusion: MISTY通过单步推理和潜空间漂移损失设计，在保持生成质量和主动规划能力的同时，大幅提升推理效率，适用于实时自动驾驶场景。

Abstract: Multi-modal trajectory generation is essential for safe autonomous driving, yet existing diffusion-based planners suffer from high inference latency due to iterative neural function evaluations. This paper presents MISTY (Mixer-based Inference for Single-step Trajectory-drifting Yield), a high-throughput generative motion planner that achieves state-of-the-art closed-loop performance with pure single-step inference. MISTY integrates a vectorized Sub-Graph encoder to capture environment context, a Variational Autoencoder to structure expert trajectories into a compact 32-dimensional latent manifold, and an ultra-lightweight MLP-Mixer decoder to eliminate quadratic attention complexity. Importantly, we introduce a latent-space drifting loss that shifts the complex distribution evolution entirely to the training phase. By formulating explicit attractive and repulsive forces, this mechanism empowers the model to synthesize novel, proactive maneuvers, such as active overtaking, that are virtually absent from the raw expert demonstrations. Extensive evaluations on the nuPlan benchmark demonstrate that MISTY achieves state-of-the-art results on the challenging Test14-hard split, with comprehensive scores of 80.32 and 82.21 in non-reactive and reactive settings, respectively. Operating at over 99 FPS with an end-to-end latency of 10.1 ms, MISTY offers an order-of-magnitude speedup over iterative diffusion planners while while achieving significantly robust generation.

</details>


### [93] [X2-N: A Transformable Wheel-legged Humanoid Robot with Dual-mode Locomotion and Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21541)
*Yan Ning,Xingzhou Chen,Delong Li,Hao Zhang,Hanfu Gai,Tongyuan Li,Cheng Zhang,Zhihui Peng,Ling Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: X2-N是一款高自由度可变形机器人，支持人形和轮腿两种模式及无缝切换，通过强化学习全身控制框架实现了混合运动、变形和操作任务的统一控制，在动态滑行、爬楼梯和包裹递送等任务中展现了高效率和强适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轮腿机器人存在两个主要局限：一是固定轮式足部和有限髋关节自由度导致腿部运动稳定性和机动性不足；二是缺乏完整上肢和手臂，限制了灵巧操作能力。

Method: 设计了高自由度可变形机器人X2-N，支持人形与轮腿双模式及关节重构切换；提出基于强化学习的全身控制框架，统一控制混合运动、变形和操作。

Result: 在动态滑行、爬楼梯和包裹递送等任务中验证，X2-N展现了高运动效率、强地形适应性和稳定的运动-操作协同性能。

Conclusion: X2-N通过高自由度设计和强化学习全身控制框架，有效提升了轮腿机器人的运动能力与操作能力，具有实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Wheel-legged robots combine the efficiency of wheeled locomotion with the versatility of legged systems, enabling rapid traversal over both continuous and discrete terrains. However, conventional designs typically employ fixed wheels as feet and limited degrees of freedom (DoFs) at the hips, resulting in reduced stability and mobility during legged locomotion compared to humanoids with flat feet. In addition, most existing platforms lack a full upper body with arms, which limits their ability to perform dexterous manipulation tasks.
  In this letter, we present X2-N, a high-DoF transformable robot with dual-mode locomotion and manipulation. X2-N can operate in both humanoid and wheel-legged forms and transform seamlessly between them through joint reconfiguration. We further propose a reinforcement learning (RL)-based whole-body control framework tailored to this morphology, enabling unified control across hybrid locomotion, transformation, and manipulation. We validate X2-N in a range of challenging locomotion and manipulation tasks, including dynamic skating-like motion, stair climbing and package delivery. Results demonstrate high locomotion efficiency, strong terrain adaptability, and stable loco-manipulation performance of X2-N, highlighting its potential for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [94] [SLAM as a Stochastic Control Problem with Partial Information: Optimal Solutions and Rigorous Approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21693)
*Ilir Gusija,Fady Alajaji,Serdar Yüksel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文从最优随机控制视角重新审视主动SLAM问题，将其重构为部分信息下的决策问题，提出了一种新的探索阶段代价函数以编码状态几何信息，并推导出具有严格理论保证的近似最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动SLAM是机器人学中的基础状态估计问题，现有方法缺乏从随机最优控制角度的严格理论分析，需要建立一个统一的理论框架来指导决策。

Method: 将主动SLAM建模为非标准的部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），引入编码状态几何信息的新探索阶段代价函数，在一般假设下分析正则性条件，推导出严格证明的近似最优解。

Result: 建立了主动SLAM的随机控制形式化框架，给出了运动、感知和地图表示的严格处理；推导了具有理论保证的近似最优解；在特定案例中通过标准学习算法验证了策略的近优性。

Conclusion: 主动SLAM可被有效重构为随机最优控制问题，新提出的代价函数和近似求解方法在理论上具有严格保证，适用于广泛的机器人应用场景。

Abstract: Simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) is a foundational state estimation problem in robotics in which a robot accurately constructs a map of its environment while also localizing itself within this construction. We study the active SLAM problem through the lens of optimal stochastic control, thereby recasting it as a decision-making problem under partial information. After reviewing several commonly studied models, we present a general stochastic control formulation of active SLAM together with a rigorous treatment of motion, sensing, and map representation. We introduce a new exploration stage cost that encodes the geometry of the state when evaluating information-gathering actions. This formulation, constructed as a nonstandard partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP), is then analyzed to derive rigorously justified approximate solutions that are near-optimal. To enable this analysis, the associated regularity conditions are studied under general assumptions that apply to a wide range of robotics applications. For a particular case, we conduct an extensive numerical study in which standard learning algorithms are used to learn near-optimal policies.

</details>


### [95] [A Compact Peristaltic Pump Based on Magneto-Elastic Hysteresis with Single Pneumatic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21729)
*Minjo Park,Metin Sitti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过单一气动输入结合嵌入式被动磁铁实现蠕动运动的软膜泵，并建立模型分析其动力学特性，通过数值模拟和实验验证了磁弹滞后行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的蠕动泵驱动方式（机械、气动、磁驱动）常导致泵体结构复杂、控制方案繁琐，需要更简化的驱动方案。

Method: 采用单一气动输入结合嵌入式被动磁铁实现软膜蠕动运动，通过建模分析简化驱动机制与系统动力学，并进行数值模拟预测内部流体流动。

Result: 数值模拟成功预测了内部流体流动和磁弹滞后行为，并通过概念验证原型实验得到了验证。

Conclusion: 所提出的软膜泵设计简化了泵体结构和控制方案，实验验证了其可行性。

Abstract: Pumping fluids is fundamental to a wide range of industrial, environmental, and biomedical applications. Among various pumping mechanisms, peristaltic pumps enable efficient and safe fluid transport by deforming an elastic tube without direct contact with the working fluid. Although previous studies have introduced mechanical, pneumatic, or magnetic actuations to drive membrane deformation, these approaches often lead to complex pump architectures and control schemes. In this study, we present a soft membrane pump that achieves peristaltic motion through a single pneumatic input combined with an embedded passive magnet. The actuation mechanism and system dynamics were analyzed and simplified through modeling. Numerical simulations were conducted to predict the internal fluid flow, and the magneto-elastic hysteresis behavior observed in the simulations was successfully validated by experiments with a proof-of-concept prototype.

</details>


### [96] [Hi-WM: Human-in-the-World-Model for Scalable Robot Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21741)
*Yaxuan Li,Zhongyi Zhou,Yefei Chen,Yanjiang Guo,Jiaming Liu,Shanghang Zhang,Jianyu Chen,Yichen Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出人-在世界模型中（Hi-WM）框架，利用世界模型作为可重用的纠正基质，通过让人类在世界模型内部直接提供短时纠正动作，实现高效的机器人后训练，在真实世界操纵任务中显著提升成功率达37.9个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练流程依赖物理执行，每次纠错都需要真实机器人、场景搭建、重置和操作员监督，成本高昂且效率低下。

Method: Hi-WM框架：1) 策略在世界模型中闭环 rollout；2) 当 rollout 出现错误或失败倾向时，人类直接在世界模型中提供短时纠正动作；3) 缓存中间状态，支持回滚和分支，使单个失败状态可复用生成多个纠正延续；4) 将纠正轨迹加入训练集进行后训练。

Result: 在三个真实世界操作任务（刚体和变形物体交互）和两个策略主干上，Hi-WM 相比基础策略平均提升37.9个百分点真实世界成功率，相比世界模型闭环基线提升19.0个百分点；世界模型评估与真实性能强相关（r=0.953）。

Conclusion: 世界模型不仅可作为生成器或评估器，还能作为有效的纠正基质，实现可扩展的机器人后训练。

Abstract: Post-training is essential for turning pretrained generalist robot policies into reliable task-specific controllers, but existing human-in-the-loop pipelines remain tied to physical execution: each correction requires robot time, scene setup, resets, and operator supervision in the real world. Meanwhile, action-conditioned world models have been studied mainly for imagination, synthetic data generation, and policy evaluation. We propose \textbf{Human-in-the-World-Model (Hi-WM)}, a post-training framework that uses a learned world model as a reusable corrective substrate for failure-targeted policy improvement. A policy is first rolled out in closed loop inside the world model; when the rollout becomes incorrect or failure-prone, a human intervenes directly in the model to provide short corrective actions. Hi-WM caches intermediate states and supports rollback and branching, allowing a single failure state to be reused for multiple corrective continuations and yielding dense supervision around behaviors that the base policy handles poorly. The resulting corrective trajectories are then added back to the training set for post-training. We evaluate Hi-WM on three real-world manipulation tasks spanning both rigid and deformable object interaction, and on two policy backbones. Hi-WM improves real-world success by 37.9 points on average over the base policy and by 19.0 points over a world-model closed-loop baseline, while world-model evaluation correlates strongly with real-world performance (r = 0.953). These results suggest that world models can serve not only as generators or evaluators, but also as effective corrective substrates for scalable robot post-training.

</details>


### [97] [Task-Driven Co-Design of Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21894)
*Maximilian Stralz,Meshal Alharbi,Yujun Huang,Gioele Zardini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于单调协同设计理论的形式化组合框架，用于任务驱动的异构多机器人系统协同设计，该框架能够联合优化机器人设计、编队组成和规划，并在多项案例研究中展示了灵活性、可扩展性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多孤立地改进机器人设计、编队组成或规划等单一领域，缺乏系统级协同设计方法，难以权衡不同域之间的耦合决策和任务需求。

Method: 基于单调协同设计理论，将机器人、编队、规划器、执行器和评估器抽象为具有良好定义接口的互联设计问题，这些抽象与具体实现和任务无关，从而实现任务驱动下的联合优化。

Result: 多项案例研究表明，该框架能够无缝集成新机器人类型、任务配置和概率感知目标，系统性地发现非直观的设计备选方案，并提供最优性保证。

Conclusion: 该形式化协同设计方法为实现复杂异构多机器人系统的原则化推理提供了灵活性、可扩展性和可解释性。

Abstract: Designing multi-agent robotic systems requires reasoning across tightly coupled decisions spanning heterogeneous domains, including robot design, fleet composition, and planning. Much effort has been devoted to isolated improvements in these domains, whereas system-level co-design considering trade-offs and task requirements remains underexplored. In this work, we present a formal and compositional framework for the task-driven co-design of heterogeneous multi-robot systems. Building on a monotone co-design theory, we introduce general abstractions of robots, fleets, planners, executors, and evaluators as interconnected design problems with well-defined interfaces that are agnostic to both implementations and tasks. This structure enables efficient joint optimization of robot design, fleet composition, and planning under task-specific performance constraints. A series of case studies demonstrates the capabilities of the framework. Various component models can be seamlessly incorporated, including new robot types, task profiles, and probabilistic sensing objectives, while non-obvious design alternatives are systematically uncovered with optimality guarantees. The results highlight the flexibility, scalability, and interpretability of the proposed approach, and illustrate how formal co-design enables principled reasoning about complex heterogeneous multi-robot systems.

</details>


### [98] [VistaBot: View-Robust Robot Manipulation via Spatiotemporal-Aware View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21914)
*Songen Gu,Yuhang Zheng,Weize Li,Yupeng Zheng,Yating Feng,Xiang Li,Yilun Chen,Pengfei Li,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VistaBot是一个结合前馈几何模型与视频扩散模型的框架，通过在动作分块(ACT)和扩散策略(π₀)上集成4D几何估计、视图合成潜在提取和潜在动作学习，实现了无需测试时相机标定的视角鲁棒闭环操作，在视图泛化分数(VGS)上分别提升了2.79倍和2.63倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端机器人操控模型在固定相机训练下对视角变化鲁棒性不足，现有方法依赖测试时相机标定，限制了实际部署中的泛化能力。

Method: 提出VistaBot框架，包含三个关键组件：4D几何估计（前馈几何模型）、视图合成潜在提取（视频扩散模型）、潜在动作学习。集成到ACT和π₀两种策略中，无需测试时相机标定。

Result: 在视图泛化分数(VGS)上，相对于ACT和π₀分别提升2.79倍和2.63倍，同时实现高质量的新视图合成。

Conclusion: 提出了一套完整的几何感知合成模型、潜在动作规划器、新的基准评估指标，并在多种环境下的仿真和真实任务中得到验证，代码和模型将开源。

Abstract: Recently, end-to-end robotic manipulation models have gained significant attention for their generalizability and scalability. However, they often suffer from limited robustness to camera viewpoint changes when training with a fixed camera. In this paper, we propose VistaBot, a novel framework that integrates feed-forward geometric models with video diffusion models to achieve view-robust closed-loop manipulation without requiring camera calibration at test time. Our approach consists of three key components: 4D geometry estimation, view synthesis latent extraction, and latent action learning. VistaBot is integrated into both action-chunking (ACT) and diffusion-based ($π_0$) policies and evaluated across simulation and real-world tasks. We further introduce the View Generalization Score (VGS) as a new metric for comprehensive evaluation of cross-view generalization. Results show that VistaBot improves VGS by 2.79$\times$ and 2.63$\times$ over ACT and $π_0$, respectively, while also achieving high-quality novel view synthesis. Our contributions include a geometry-aware synthesis model, a latent action planner, a new benchmark metric, and extensive validation across diverse environments. The code and models will be made publicly available.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [99] [Frequency-Forcing: From Scaling-as-Time to Soft Frequency Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20902)
*Weitao Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Frequency-Forcing通过可学习的小波包变换将硬频率约束转化为软引导机制，在不改变核心流坐标的前提下实现了尺度有序生成，在ImageNet-256上持续优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准流匹配模型均匀传输噪声到数据，而显式引入生成顺序（先粗低频结构后细细节）对自然图像合成非常有效。本文旨在结合K-Flow的硬频率约束和Latent Forcing的软引导机制，提出一种无需外部依赖、保留像素流路径的频率有序生成方法。

Method: 提出Frequency-Forcing方法：用轻量级可学习小波包变换从数据自身导出低频辅助流（自强制信号），该辅助流比主像素流更早成熟，通过Latent Forcing的异步时间调度机制引导标准像素流实现频率有序生成，避免了K-Flow的硬约束改写和Latent Forcing对重预训练编码器的依赖。

Result: 在ImageNet-256上，Frequency-Forcing持续改进了FID指标，优于强像素空间和潜空间基线，并且能与语义流自然组合获得进一步提升。

Conclusion: 基于强制机制的尺度有序生成是一种通用的、路径保持的替代方案，相比于硬频率流更具灵活性。

Abstract: While standard flow-matching models transport noise to data uniformly, incorporating an explicit generation order - specifically, establishing coarse, low-frequency structure before fine detail - has proven highly effective for synthesizing natural images. Two recent works offer distinct paradigms for this. K-Flow imposes a hard frequency constraint by reinterpreting a frequency scaling variable as flow time, running the trajectory inside a transformed amplitude space. Latent Forcing provides a soft ordering mechanism by coupling the pixel flow with an auxiliary semantic latent flow via asynchronous time schedules, leaving the pixel interpolation path itself untouched. Viewed from the angle of improving pixel generation, we observe that forcing - guiding generation with an earlier-maturing auxiliary stream - offers a highly compatible route to scale-ordered generation without rewriting the core flow coordinate. Building on this, we propose Frequency-Forcing, which realizes K-Flow's frequency ordering through Latent Forcing's soft mechanism: a standard pixel flow is guided by an auxiliary low-frequency stream that matures earlier in time. Unlike Latent Forcing, whose scratchpad relies on a heavy pretrained encoder (e.g., DINO), our frequency scratchpad is derived from the data itself via a lightweight learnable wavelet packet transform. We term this a self-forcing signal, which avoids external dependencies while learning a basis better adapted to data statistics than the fixed bases used in hard frequency flows. On ImageNet-256, Frequency-Forcing consistently improves FID over strong pixel- and latent-space baselines, and naturally composes with a semantic stream to yield further gains. This illustrates that forcing-based scale ordering is a versatile, path-preserving alternative to hard frequency flows.

</details>


### [100] [Quotient-Space Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21809)
*Yixian Xu,Yusong Wang,Shengjie Luo,Kaiyuan Gao,Tianyu He,Di He,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个通用的商空间（quotient space）扩散建模框架，专门处理具有对称性的生成任务，并将其应用于具有SE(3)对称性的分子结构生成。该框架通过避免学习与群作用对应的分量来简化学习难度，并保证采样器能恢复目标分布，在分子和蛋白质结构生成任务上优于传统对称性处理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在科学领域（如分子3D结构生成）中应用广泛。许多任务存在内在对称性（如SE(3)对称性），即系统在群作用下等价。传统方法采用群等变扩散模型，但需要学习群作用对应的分量，增加了学习难度；而启发式对齐策略缺乏合适的采样器。因此需要建立一个在商空间上进行扩散建模的形式化框架。

Method: 建立了通用商空间上的扩散模型形式化框架，将目标分布定义在群作用下的商空间上，从而避免学习与群作用对应的分量。该框架保证采样器能够恢复目标分布。

Result: 在小分子和蛋白质结构生成任务上进行了实证验证，结果表明商空间扩散模型优于以往的对称性处理方法。

Conclusion: 商空间扩散模型为具有对称性的生成任务提供了一个原则性的新框架，能够简化学习难度并保证采样正确性，在分子结构生成中表现优于传统方法。

Abstract: Diffusion-based generative models have reformed generative AI, and have enabled new capabilities in the science domain, for example, generating 3D structures of molecules. Due to the intrinsic problem structure of certain tasks, there is often a symmetry in the system, which identifies objects that can be converted by a group action as equivalent, hence the target distribution is essentially defined on the quotient space with respect to the group. In this work, we establish a formal framework for diffusion modeling on a general quotient space, and apply it to molecular structure generation which follows the special Euclidean group $\text{SE}(3)$ symmetry. The framework reduces the necessity of learning the component corresponding to the group action, hence simplifies learning difficulty over conventional group-equivariant diffusion models, and the sampler guarantees recovering the target distribution, while heuristic alignment strategies lack proper samplers. The arguments are empirically validated on structure generation for small molecules and proteins, indicating that the principled quotient-space diffusion model provides a new framework that outperforms previous symmetry treatments.

</details>


### [101] [Do Masked Autoencoders Improve Downhole Prediction? An Empirical Study on Real Well Drilling Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20909)
*Aleksander Berezowski,Hassan Hassanzadeh,Gouri Ginde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将掩码自编码器（MAE）预训练应用于井下钻井指标预测，在72种配置的系统性实验中，最佳MAE相比监督GRU基线降低19.8%的测试MAE，但比监督LSTM基线高6.4%。研究发现潜在空间宽度是主导架构因素，而掩码比率影响可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 井下钻井遥测存在根本性的标签不对称问题：地面传感器数据连续生成（1 Hz），而带标签的井下测量数据成本高、间歇且稀缺。现有方法普遍采用全监督从头训练，不适合这种数据分布。

Method: 使用Utah FORGE两口地热井约350万时间步的多变量钻井遥测数据，在全因子设计空间中对72种MAE配置进行系统搜索，并与监督LSTM和GRU基线在总泥浆体积预测任务上比较。

Result: 最佳MAE配置相对监督GRU基线降低19.8%测试MAE，但比监督LSTM基线高6.4%。潜在空间宽度与测试MAE的Pearson相关系数为-0.59，是主导架构因素；掩码比率影响可忽略（归因于1 Hz钻井数据的高时间冗余性）。

Conclusion: MAE预训练是钻井分析中可行的范式，本文识别了其最有利的应用条件。

Abstract: Downhole drilling telemetry presents a fundamental labeling asymmetry: surface sensor data are generated continuously at 1~Hz, while labeled downhole measurements are costly, intermittent, and scarce. Current machine learning approaches for downhole metric prediction universally adopt fully supervised training from scratch, which is poorly suited to this data regime. We present the first empirical evaluation of masked autoencoder (MAE) pretraining for downhole drilling metric prediction. Using two publicly available Utah FORGE geothermal wells comprising approximately 3.5 million timesteps of multivariate drilling telemetry, we conduct a systematic full-factorial design space search across 72 MAE configurations and compare them against supervised LSTM and GRU baselines on the task of predicting Total Mud Volume. Results show that the best MAE configuration reduces test mean absolute error by 19.8\% relative to the supervised GRU baseline, while trailing the supervised LSTM baseline by 6.4\%. Analysis of design dimensions reveals that latent space width is the dominant architectural choice (Pearson $r = -0.59$ with test MAE), while masking ratio has negligible effect, an unexpected finding attributed to high temporal redundancy in 1~Hz drilling data. These results establish MAE pretraining as a viable paradigm for drilling analytics and identify the conditions under which it is most beneficial.

</details>


### [102] [FairyFuse: Multiplication-Free LLM Inference on CPUs via Fused Ternary Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20913)
*Fei Zuo,Xiaoyan Xi,Quanyi Zeng,Feiyu Wang,Ho Fai Leung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FairyFuse 是一个基于 CPU 的推理系统，通过将三元权重（{-1,0,+1}）与 AVX-512 掩码加/减指令融合，实现了无浮点乘法的 LLM 推理，相比 llama.cpp Q4_K_M 取得 1.24x 加速，达到 32.4 tokens/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPU 平台上 LLM 推理受限于内存带宽，现有 4-bit 量化仍需反量化并执行浮点乘法，未能充分利用更低比特量化的计算效率优势。

Method: 将三元权重（{-1,0,+1}）的每个线性层中的 8 个实值子 GEMV 融合为单个 AVX-512 循环，仅使用掩码加法和减法操作，完全消除浮点乘法；利用 16 倍权重压缩将内存受限的 GEMV 推向计算受限区域。

Result: 在单颗 Intel Xeon 8558P 上达到 32.4 tokens/s，比 llama.cpp Q4_K_M 快 1.24x；内核加速比 29.6x；WikiText-2 困惑度 5.52（FP16 为 5.47），下游准确率 66.0%，实现近无损质量。

Conclusion: 三元权重结合 AVX-512 掩码操作可在带宽受限的 CPU 上实现无乘法 LLM 推理，显著提升吞吐量且保持模型质量，但该加速策略对 GPU 收益有限。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed on CPU-only platforms where memory bandwidth is the primary bottleneck for autoregressive generation. Weight quantization to four bits or below reduces memory pressure, yet existing systems still dequantize weights and perform floating-point multiplications, limiting the achievable gains. Ternary weights in {-1, 0, +1} provide a more efficient alternative, replacing multiplications with conditional additions, subtractions, or no-ops. While Fairy2i shows that ternary LLMs can match FP16 quality, its runtime does not exploit this structure. We present FairyFuse, an inference system that enables multiplication-free execution on commodity CPUs by fusing the eight real-valued sub-GEMVs of each widely-linear layer into a single AVX-512 loop using masked additions and subtractions, with zero floating-point multiplications. Roofline analysis shows that 16x weight compression shifts memory-bound GEMV toward the compute regime on bandwidth-limited CPUs, yielding a 29.6x kernel speedup while offering little benefit on GPUs. End-to-end, FairyFuse achieves 32.4 tokens per second on a single Intel Xeon 8558P, outperforming llama.cpp Q4_K_M by 1.24x with near-lossless quality (WikiText-2 perplexity 5.52 vs. 5.47 FP16; downstream accuracy 66.0%).

</details>


### [103] [Forget, Then Recall: Learnable Compression and Selective Unfolding via Gist Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20920)
*Yuzhen Mao,Michael Y. Li,Emily B. Fox*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GSA（Gist Sparse Attention），通过可学习的gist压缩令牌将KV缓存选择与压缩技术桥接，实现端到端的稀疏注意力机制。该方法先压缩上下文为gist令牌，选择最相关的gist，再恢复对应原始块进行细粒度注意力，并支持分层递归gist-of-gist构建，实现多分辨率上下文访问和对数级解码复杂度。在LongBench和RAG基准上，该方法在8倍到32倍压缩比下一致优于其他压缩基线和推理时稀疏注意力方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展大语言模型到长上下文面临全注意力的二次计算成本挑战，现有关键值缓存选择或压缩技术各有局限，缺乏一种端到端可学习且无需修改架构的有效桥梁。

Method: 提出GSA（Gist Sparse Attention），核心是将交错gist压缩令牌作为稀疏注意力的路由信号。具体包括：1）选择性展开机制——先压缩上下文为gist令牌，选择最相关gist，再恢复对应原始块进行细粒度注意力；2）端到端训练，无需外部检索模块；3）分层递归gist-of-gist构建，支持多分辨率上下文访问和对数级解码复杂度。

Result: 在LongBench和RAG基准上，GSA在8倍到32倍压缩比下一致优于其他压缩基线（如压缩方法）和推理时稀疏注意力方法。

Conclusion: GSA作为一种简单而有效的粗到细机制，结合了紧凑全局表示与针对性细粒度证据访问，为长上下文LLM提供了一种实用的稀疏注意力方案。

Abstract: Scaling large language models to long contexts is challenging due to the quadratic computational cost of full attention. Mitigation approaches include KV-cache selection or compression techniques. We instead provide an effective and end-to-end learnable bridge between the two without requiring architecture modification. In particular, our key insight is that interleaved gist compression tokens -- which provide a learnable summary of sets of raw tokens -- can serve as routing signals for sparse attention. Building on this, we introduce selective unfolding via GSA, which first compresses the context into gist tokens, then selects the most relevant gists, and subsequently restores the corresponding raw chunks for detailed attention. This yields a simple coarse-to-fine mechanism that combines compact global representations with targeted access to fine-grained evidence. We further incorporate this process directly into training in an end-to-end fashion, avoiding the need for external retrieval modules. In addition, we extend the framework hierarchically via recursive gist-of-gist construction, enabling multi-resolution context access with logarithmic per-step decoding complexity. Empirical results on LongBench and RAG benchmarks demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms other compression baselines as well as inference-time sparse attention methods across compression ratios from $8\times$ to $32\times$. The code is available at: https://github.com/yuzhenmao/gist-sparse-attention/

</details>


### [104] [Validating a Deep Learning Algorithm to Identify Patients with Glaucoma using Systemic Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20921)
*John Xiang,Rohith Ravindranath,Sophia Y. Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用All of Us国家数据训练的青光眼风险评估模型，仅通过系统性电子健康记录（无需专科影像）即可在独立机构识别高风险患者，最佳模型AUROC达0.883，PPV为0.657，最高预测十分位数组的青光眼诊断率（65.7%）和治疗率（57.0%）最高，性能随可训练层数（最多15层）和数据量增加而提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否可以利用仅包含系统性电子健康记录（非专科眼科影像）的青光眼风险评估模型，在独立医疗机构中识别出青光眼高概率患者，以实现可扩展、可及的大规模预筛查。

Method: 横断面研究，纳入2013年11月至2024年1月期间斯坦福医疗中心的20,636名患者（15%患青光眼），对预训练的GRA模型进行微调，输入特征包括人口统计学、系统性诊断、用药、实验室检查和体格检查测量，在留出测试集上评估性能。

Result: 最佳模型AUROC为0.883，PPV为0.657；最高预测十分位数组青光眼诊断率65.7%、治疗率57.0%；模型性能随可训练层数（最多15层）和数据量增加而提升。

Conclusion: 仅基于电子健康记录的青光眼风险评估模型可能实现无需专科影像的可扩展、可及预筛查，校准结果与临床风险一致。

Abstract: We evaluated whether a glaucoma risk assessment (GRA) model trained on All of Us national data can identify patients at high probability of glaucoma using only systemic electronic health records (EHR) at an independent institution. In this cross-sectional study, 20,636 Stanford patients seen from November 2013 to January 2024 were included (15% with glaucoma). A pretrained GRA model was fine-tuned on the Stanford cohort and tested on a held-out set using demographics, systemic diagnoses, medications, laboratory results, and physical examination measurements as inputs. The best model achieved AUROC 0.883 and PPV 0.657. Calibration was consistent with clinical risk: the highest prediction decile showed the greatest glaucoma diagnosis rate (65.7%) and treatment rate (57.0%). Performance improved with more trainable layers up to 15 and with additional data. An EHR-only GRA model may enable scalable and accessible pre-screening without specialized imaging.

</details>


### [105] [ILDR: Geometric Early Detection of Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20923)
*Shreel Golwala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出类间/类内距离比（ILDR）作为Grokking现象的早期检测信号，该指标基于倒数第二层表示的几何结构，能够在验证准确率提升前检测到表征空间的重组，并可作为早停触发和优化器干预的依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Grokking检测信号（如权重范数、GrokFast的梯度EMA）存在滞后性或不稳定问题，缺乏一个直接、稳定的早期检测指标来捕捉泛化发生前的表征空间变化。

Method: 提出ILDR指标，计算倒数第二层表示的类间质心距离与类内散度的比值；基于Fisher线性判别准则，无需特征分解，复杂度为O(|C|^2 + N)；在留出数据上计算以排除记忆化影响。

Result: 在模算术和置换群组合（S5）任务上，ILDR领先Grokking转变9%-73%的训练预算，平均领先950±250步（变异系数26%）；后Grokking方差下降1696倍；作为早停触发平均减少18.6%训练；优化器干预可双向控制转变。

Conclusion: ILDR是一个有效的Grokking早期检测信号，能够反映泛化所需的表征空间条件而非下游相关性，可用于早停和可控干预。

Abstract: Grokking describes a delayed generalization phenomenon in which a neural network achieves perfect training accuracy long before validation accuracy improves, followed by an abrupt transition to strong generalization. Existing detection signals are indirect: weight norm reflects parameter-space regularization and consistently lags the transition, while GrokFast's slow gradient EMA, used without gradient amplification, is unstable across seeds with standard deviation exceeding mean lead time. We propose the Inter/Intra-class Distance Ratio (ILDR), a geometric metric computed on second-to-last layer representations as the ratio of inter-class centroid separation to intra-class scatter. ILDR provides an early detection signal: it rises and crosses a threshold at 2.5 times its baseline before the grokking transition appears in validation accuracy, indicating early geometric reorganization in representation space. Grounded in Fisher's linear discriminant criterion, ILDR requires no eigendecomposition and runs in O(|C|^2 + N). It is evaluated exclusively on held-out data, making it robust to memorization effects. Across modular arithmetic and permutation group composition (S5), ILDR leads the grokking transition by 9 to 73 percent of the training budget, with lead time increasing with task algebraic complexity. Over eight random seeds, ILDR leads by 950 +/- 250 steps with a coefficient of variation of 26 percent, and post-grokking variance drops by 1696 times, consistent with a sharp phase transition in representation space. Using ILDR as an early stopping trigger reduces training by 18.6 percent on average. Optimizer interventions triggered at the ILDR threshold demonstrate bidirectional control over the transition, suggesting ILDR tracks representational conditions underlying generalization rather than a downstream correlate.

</details>


### [106] [Clinically Interpretable Sepsis Early Warning via LLM-Guided Simulation of Temporal Physiological Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20924)
*Weizhi Nie,Zhen Qu,Weijie Wang,Chunpei Li,Ke Lu,Bingyang Zhou,Hongzhi Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种LLM引导的时间模拟框架，通过显式模拟脓毒症发作前的生理轨迹，实现可解释的早期脓毒症预警，在MIMIC-IV和eICU数据库上AUC达0.861-0.903，优于传统深度学习和基于规则的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据驱动的脓毒症早期预警模型虽然准确，但预测过程不透明，限制了临床医生的信任度和应用可行性。亟需一种既能保持高预测性能，又能提供可解释预测机制的方法。

Method: 提出LLM引导的时间模拟框架，包含三个核心模块：1) 时空特征提取模块，捕获多变量生命体征的动态依赖关系；2) 医学提示作为前缀模块，将临床推理线索嵌入LLM；3) 基于智能体的后处理组件，将预测约束在生理合理范围内。先模拟关键生理指标的演变，再分类脓毒症发作。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV和eICU数据库上，该方法在24小时至4小时预发作预测任务中AUC达0.861-0.903，优于传统深度学习和基于规则的基线方法。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了可解释的生理轨迹和风险趋势，能够辅助临床医生进行早期干预和个性化决策，兼具高预测性能和临床可解释性。

Abstract: Timely and interpretable early warning of sepsis remains a major clinical challenge due to the complex temporal dynamics of physiological deterioration. Traditional data-driven models often provide accurate yet opaque predictions, limiting physicians' confidence and clinical applicability. To address this limitation, we propose a Large Language Model (LLM)-guided temporal simulation framework that explicitly models physiological trajectories prior to disease onset for clinically interpretable prediction. The framework consists of a spatiotemporal feature extraction module that captures dynamic dependencies among multivariate vital signs, a Medical Prompt-as-Prefix module that embeds clinical reasoning cues into LLMs, and an agent-based post-processing component that constrains predictions within physiologically plausible ranges. By first simulating the evolution of key physiological indicators and then classifying sepsis onset, our model offers transparent prediction mechanisms that align with clinical judgment. Evaluated on the MIMIC-IV and eICU databases, the proposed method achieves superior AUC scores (0.861-0.903) across 24-4-hour pre-onset prediction tasks, outperforming conventional deep learning and rule-based approaches. More importantly, it provides interpretable trajectories and risk trends that can assist clinicians in early intervention and personalized decision-making in intensive care environments.

</details>


### [107] [Domain-Aware Hierarchical Contrastive Learning for Semi-Supervised Generalization Fault Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20928)
*Junyu Ren,Wensheng Gan,Philip S Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种域感知层次对比学习框架（DAHCL），用于解决半监督域泛化故障诊断中伪标签偏差和不确定样本利用不足的问题。该框架包含域感知学习模块（缓解跨域伪标签偏差）和层次对比学习模块（通过动态置信度分层与模糊对比监督利用不确定样本），并在含工程噪声的评估协议上展示了优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半监督域泛化故障诊断方法存在两个耦合问题：一是为未标注域生成的伪标签主要依赖标注源域知识，忽略了域特定的几何差异，导致系统性跨域伪标签偏差；二是对未标注样本采用硬性的接受或丢弃策略，刚性阈值导致跨域样本利用不均衡，且对不确定样本的硬标签分配容易引入额外噪声。

Method: 提出DAHCL统一框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 域感知学习（DAL）模块，显式捕获源域几何特征并校准跨异构源域的伪标签预测，缓解伪标签生成中的跨域偏差；(2) 层次对比学习（HCL）模块，结合动态置信度分层与模糊对比监督，使不确定样本无需依赖不可靠硬标签即可参与表示学习。此外，将工程噪声纳入SSDGFD评估协议以更好地反映实际工业场景。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的大量实验证明了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: DAHCL通过联合改进监督质量和未标注样本利用率，有效解决了半监督域泛化故障诊断中的伪标签偏差和样本利用不均衡问题。

Abstract: Fault diagnosis under unseen operating conditions remains highly challenging when labeled data are scarce. Semi-supervised domain generalization fault diagnosis (SSDGFD) provides a practical solution by jointly exploiting labeled and unlabeled source domains. However, existing methods still suffer from two coupled limitations. First, pseudo-labels for unlabeled domains are typically generated primarily from knowledge learned on the labeled source domain, which neglects domain-specific geometric discrepancies and thus induces systematic cross-domain pseudo-label bias. Second, unlabeled samples are commonly handled with a hard accept-or-discard strategy, where rigid thresholding causes imbalanced sample utilization across domains, while hard-label assignment for uncertain samples can easily introduce additional noise. To address these issues, we propose a unified framework termed domain-aware hierarchical contrastive learning (DAHCL) for SSDGFD. Specifically, DAHCL introduces a domain-aware learning (DAL) module to explicitly capture source-domain geometric characteristics and calibrate pseudo-label predictions across heterogeneous source domains, thereby mitigating cross-domain bias in pseudo-label generation. In addition, DAHCL develops a hierarchical contrastive learning (HCL) module that combines dynamic confidence stratification with fuzzy contrastive supervision, enabling uncertain samples to contribute to representation learning without relying on unreliable hard labels. In this way, DAHCL jointly improves the quality of supervision and the utilization of unlabeled samples. Furthermore, to better reflect practical industrial scenarios, we incorporate engineering noise into the SSDGFD evaluation protocol. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that...

</details>


### [108] [Data-Driven Open-Loop Simulation for Digital-Twin Operator Decision Support in Wastewater Treatment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20935)
*Gary Simethy,Daniel Ortiz Arroyo,Petar Durdevic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了CCSS-RS，一种用于污水处理厂数字孪生的受控连续时间状态空间模型，在高度不完整和不规则采样的全规模数据上，相比基线方法将RMSE降低40-46%，并展示了操作价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 污水处理厂需要数字孪生决策支持工具，能够模拟控制计划下的工厂响应、容忍不规则和缺失的传感数据，并在12-36小时规划周期内保持信息有效性。用全规模工厂数据满足这些需求仍然是一个开放的工程-AI挑战。

Method: CCSS-RS将历史状态推断与未来控制和外部输入 rollout 分离。模型结合了类型化上下文编码、增益加权的强制驱动（预设和预测驱动）、半群一致 rollout，以及用于处理重尾和零膨胀WWTP传感器数据的Student-t加hurdle输出。

Result: 在Avedøre全规模基准上（906,815时间步，43%缺失率，1-20分钟不规则采样），CCSS-RS在H=1000时达到RMSE 0.696和CRPS 0.349。相比Neural CDE基线RMSE降低40-46%，相比简化内部变体降低31-35%。四个案例研究验证了操作价值。

Conclusion: CCSS-RS可作为工业污水处理中离线场景筛选的实用学习模拟器，与机理模型互补。

Abstract: Wastewater treatment plants (WWTPs) need digital-twin-style decision support tools that can simulate plant response under prescribed control plans, tolerate irregular and missing sensing, and remain informative over 12-36 h planning horizons. Meeting these requirements with full-scale plant data remains an open engineering-AI challenge. We present CCSS-RS, a controlled continuous-time state-space model that separates historical state inference from future control and exogenous rollout. The model combines typed context encoding, gain-weighted forcing of prescribed and forecast drivers, semigroup-consistent rollouts, and Student-t plus hurdle outputs for heavy-tailed and zero-inflated WWTP sensor data. On the public Avedøre full-scale benchmark, with 906,815 timesteps, 43% missingness, and 1-20 min irregular sampling, CCSS-RS achieves RMSE 0.696 and CRPS 0.349 at H=1000 across 10,000 test windows. This reduces RMSE by 40-46% relative to Neural CDE baselines and by 31-35% relative to simplified internal variants. Four case studies using a frozen checkpoint on test data demonstrate operational value: oxygen-setpoint perturbations shift predicted ammonium by -2.3 to +1.4 over horizons 300-1000; a smoothed setpoint plan ranks first in multi-criterion screening; context-only sensor outages raise monitored-variable RMSE by at most 10%; and ammonium, nitrate, and oxygen remain more accurate than persistence throughout the rollout. These results establish CCSS-RS as a practical learned simulator for offline scenario screening in industrial wastewater treatment, complementary to mechanistic models.

</details>


### [109] [Sink-Token-Aware Pruning for Fine-Grained Video Understanding in Efficient Video LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20937)
*Kibum Kim,Jiwan Kim,Kyle Min,Yueqi Wang,Jinyoung Moon,Julian McAuley,Chanyoung Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了现有视频大语言模型的无训练视觉令牌剪枝方法在细粒度理解任务上性能严重下降，并发现"汇令牌"（sink tokens）是主要障碍。作者提出SToP方法，通过汇分数量化并抑制汇令牌，在剪枝高达90%视觉令牌时仍能显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频LLM推理延迟高，训练-free视觉令牌剪枝方法在MCQA上有效，但在需要精确视觉定位的细粒度理解任务（如幻觉评估）中性能严重下降，需探究原因并提出解决方案。

Method: 提出SToP（Sink-Token-aware Pruning），引入sink score量化每个令牌作为汇令牌的倾向性，将该分数应用于现有空间和时间剪枝方法中抑制汇令牌，是一种即插即用的方法。

Result: 将SToP应用于VisionZip、FastVid、Holitom等最先进剪枝方法，在幻觉评估、开放生成、组合推理和MCQA等多类基准上验证，即使在剪枝90%视觉令牌时仍显著提升性能。

Conclusion: 汇令牌是阻碍细粒度视频理解的关键因素，SToP通过感知并抑制汇令牌，有效提升了视频LLM在细粒度理解任务上的表现，为训练-free视觉令牌剪枝提供了新方向。

Abstract: Video Large Language Models (Video LLMs) incur high inference latency due to a large number of visual tokens provided to LLMs. To address this, training-free visual token pruning has emerged as a solution to reduce computational costs; however, existing methods are primarily validated on Multiple-Choice Question Answering (MCQA) benchmarks, where coarse-grained cues often suffice. In this work, we reveal that these methods suffer a sharp performance collapse on fine-grained understanding tasks requiring precise visual grounding, such as hallucination evaluation. To explore this gap, we conduct a systematic analysis and identify sink tokens--semantically uninformative tokens that attract excessive attention--as a key obstacle to fine-grained video understanding. When these sink tokens survive pruning, they distort the model's visual evidence and hinder fine-grained understanding. Motivated by these insights, we propose Sink-Token-aware Pruning (SToP), a simple yet effective plug-and-play method that introduces a sink score to quantify each token's tendency to behave as a sink and applies this score to existing spatial and temporal pruning methods to suppress them, thereby enhancing video understanding. To validate the effectiveness of SToP, we apply it to state-of-the-art pruning methods (VisionZip, FastVid, and Holitom) and evaluate it across diverse benchmarks covering hallucination, open-ended generation, compositional reasoning, and MCQA. Our results demonstrate that SToP significantly boosts performance, even when pruning up to 90% of visual tokens.

</details>


### [110] [SCM: Sleep-Consolidated Memory with Algorithmic Forgetting for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20943)
*Saish Sachin Shinde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCM是一个受神经科学启发的LLM记忆架构，通过工作记忆、重要性标记、睡眠阶段巩固、基于价值的遗忘和自我模型五个核心组件，在保持完美回忆准确率的同时减少90.9%的记忆噪声，搜索延迟低于1毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM系统缺乏持久、结构化且生物学上合理的记忆机制，依赖截断上下文窗口、无界增长的向量数据库或缺乏巩固与遗忘机制的层级存储系统，无法有效解决记忆持久性问题。

Method: 提出SCM架构，包含五个核心组件：有限容量的工作记忆、多维重要性标记、离线睡眠阶段巩固（NREM和REM阶段）、基于意图的价值性遗忘以及支持内省的计算自我模型。

Result: 在八项标准化基准测试中，原型系统在十轮对话上达到完美回忆准确率，通过自适应遗忘将记忆噪声降低90.9%，即使存储数百个概念，记忆搜索延迟仍低于1毫秒。

Conclusion: 该工作为能够巩固、优先排序和遗忘的记忆系统建立了架构基础，为推进LLM记忆研究提供了可测试的平台。

Abstract: We present SCM (Sleep-Consolidated Memory), a research preview of a memory architecture for large language models that draws on neuroscientific principles to address a fundamental limitation in current systems: the absence of persistent, structured, and biologically plausible memory. Existing approaches rely on truncating context windows, growing vector databases without bound, or tiered storage systems that lack consolidation and forgetting mechanisms. SCM implements five core components inspired by human memory: a limited-capacity working memory, multi-dimensional importance tagging, offline sleep-stage consolidation with distinct NREM and REM phases, intentional value-based forgetting, and a computational self-model enabling introspection. Across a standardized benchmark suite of eight tests, the prototype achieves perfect recall accuracy over ten-turn conversations while reducing memory noise by 90.9% through adaptive forgetting. Memory search latency remains below one millisecond even with hundreds of stored concepts. This work establishes the architectural foundations for memory systems that consolidate, prioritize, and forget, offering a testable platform for advancing LLM memory research.

</details>


### [111] [LAF-Based Evaluation and UTTL-Based Learning Strategies with MIATTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20944)
*Yongquan Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对真实目标无法精确定义的ML场景，提出并实现了EL-MIATT框架下的LAF逻辑评估算法和UTTL学习策略，从理论和实践上桥接了不确定监督下的逻辑语义与统计优化，为"真实标签"固有不确定的ML系统提供了原则性基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多真实ML应用中，真实目标因模糊性或主观性而无法精确定义，传统假设"真实标签客观存在"的范式不适用，亟需新的理论框架和实用方法。

Method: 提出两个互补机制：基于LAF（逻辑评估公式）的评估算法（可直接在原始MIATTs或合成的三元目标上运行）和基于UTTL（不可定义真实目标学习）的学习策略（使用Dice和交叉熵损失，比较逐目标和聚合优化方案），并分析MIATTs的覆盖率和多样性对结构属性及下游任务的影响。

Result: LAF评估算法在可解释性、合理性和完整性之间取得平衡；UTTL学习策略提供了基于多个不精确真实目标的训练方案；两者的集成桥接了逻辑语义与统计优化之间的鸿沟。

Conclusion: 该工作为"真实标签"固有不确定的ML系统提供了实现EL-MIATT框架的连贯路径，建立了不确定监督下逻辑一致且实际可行的建模基础。

Abstract: In many real-world machine learning (ML) applications, the true target cannot be precisely defined due to ambiguity or subjectivity information. To address this challenge, under the assumption that the true target for a given ML task is not assumed to exist objectively in the real world, the EL-MIATTs (Evaluation and Learning with Multiple Inaccurate True Targets) framework has been proposed. Bridging theory and practice in implementing EL-MIATTs, in this paper, we develop two complementary mechanisms: LAF (Logical Assessment Formula)-based evaluation algorithms and UTTL (Undefinable True Target Learning)-based learning strategies with MIATTs, which together enable logically coherent and practically feasible modeling under uncertain supervision. We first analyze task-specific MIATTs, examining how their coverage and diversity determine their structural property and influence downstream evaluation and learning. Based on this understanding, we formulate LAF-grounded evaluation algorithms that operate either on original MIATTs or on ternary targets synthesized from them, balancing interpretability, soundness, and completeness. For model training, we introduce UTTL-grounded learning strategies using Dice and cross-entropy loss functions, comparing per-target and aggregated optimization schemes. We also discuss how the integration of LAF and UTTL bridges the gap between logical semantics and statistical optimization. Together, these components provide a coherent pathway for implementing EL-MIATTs, offering a principled foundation for developing ML systems in scenarios where the notion of "ground truth" is inherently uncertain. An application of this work's results is presented as part of the study available at https://www.qeios.com/read/EZWLSN.

</details>


### [112] [Differentially Private Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20985)
*Qichuan Yin,Manzil Zaheer,Tian Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种后处理技术（随机选择和线性组合），利用一组已训练好的、具有不同隐私/效用权衡的模型，在不额外训练的情况下生成满足任意目标差分隐私要求的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习应用中，推理或部署时的隐私要求会因政策、法规或用户体验的变化而不断变化，现有模型无法灵活调整隐私参数，需要一种无需重新训练即可满足任意差分隐私要求的方案。

Method: 提出两种后处理技术：随机选择和线性组合，基于一组在相同数据集上训练、具有不同隐私/效用权衡的现有模型，输出满足任意目标隐私参数的最终模型。使用Rényi差分隐私和隐私损失分布进行隐私核算。

Result: 在私有均值估计案例中，完整刻画了隐私/效用结果，理论上证明了线性组合优于随机选择；在多种模型及合成与真实数据集上进行了实验验证。

Conclusion: 所提出的后处理方法能够在不额外训练的情况下灵活满足变化的差分隐私需求，其中线性组合方法在理论上和实验上均表现出更优的性能。

Abstract: In machine learning applications, privacy requirements during inference or deployment time could change constantly due to varying policies, regulations, or user experience. In this work, we aim to generate a magnitude of models to satisfy any target differential privacy (DP) requirement without additional training steps, given a set of existing models trained on the same dataset with different privacy/utility tradeoffs. We propose two post processing techniques, namely random selection and linear combination, to output a final private model for any target privacy parameter. We provide privacy accounting of these approaches from the lens of R'enyi DP and privacy loss distributions for general problems. In a case study on private mean estimation, we fully characterize the privacy/utility results and theoretically establish the superiority of linear combination over random selection. Empirically, we validate our approach and analyses on several models and both synthetic and real-world datasets.

</details>


### [113] [SGD at the Edge of Stability: The Stochastic Sharpness Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21016)
*Fangshuo Liao,Afroditi Kolomvaki,Anastasios Kyrillidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了随机自稳定化框架来解释小批量SGD中锐度被抑制在2/η以下的现象，推导出平衡锐度间隙的闭式公式ΔS = ηβσ_u²/(4α)，表明更小的批量大小产生更平坦的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GD全批梯度下降中锐度稳定在2/η的EoS现象已被解释，但小批量SGD中锐度稳定在2/η以下且间隙随批量减小而增大的现象缺乏理论解释。

Method: 将自稳定化框架扩展到SGD，利用梯度噪声向顶部Hessian特征向量方向注入方差，增强三次锐度减小力，使平衡点下移。定义相对于移动投影梯度下降轨迹的随机预测动力学，证明随机耦合定理限制SGD偏差。

Result: 推导出闭式平衡锐度间隙ΔS = ηβσ_u²/(4α)，其中α为渐进锐化率，β为自稳定化强度，σ_u²为投影到顶部特征向量的梯度噪声方差。

Conclusion: 该公式预测更小的批量产生更平坦的解，当批量等于全数据集时恢复GD结果。

Abstract: When training neural networks with full-batch gradient descent (GD) and step size $η$, the largest eigenvalue of the Hessian -- the sharpness $S(\boldsymbolθ)$ -- rises to $2/η$ and hovers there, a phenomenon termed the Edge of Stability (EoS). \citet{damian2023selfstab} showed that this behavior is explained by a self-stabilization mechanism driven by third-order structure of the loss, and that GD implicitly follows projected gradient descent (PGD) on the constraint $ S(\boldsymbolθ)\leq 2/η$. For mini-batch stochastic gradient descent (SGD), the sharpness stabilizes below $2/η$, with the gap widening as the batch size decreases; yet no theoretical explanation exists for this suppression.
  We introduce stochastic self-stabilization, extending the self-stabilization framework to SGD. Our key insight is that gradient noise injects variance into the oscillatory dynamics along the top Hessian eigenvector, strengthening the cubic sharpness-reducing force and shifting the equilibrium below $2/η$. Following the approach of \citet{damian2023selfstab}, we define stochastic predicted dynamics relative to a moving projected gradient descent trajectory and prove a stochastic coupling theorem that bounds the deviation of SGD from these predictions. We derive a closed-form equilibrium sharpness gap: $ΔS = ηβσ_{\boldsymbol{u}}^{2}/(4α)$, where $α$ is the progressive sharpening rate, $β$ is the self-stabilization strength, and $σ_{ \boldsymbol{u}}^{2}$ is the gradient noise variance projected onto the top eigenvector. This formula predicts that smaller batch sizes yield flatter solutions and recovers GD when the batch equals the full dataset.

</details>


### [114] [MCAP: Deployment-Time Layer Profiling for Memory-Constrained LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21026)
*Anurita Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MCAP是一种逐层重要性估计方法，结合NVE系统，可在异构硬件上根据内存预算动态调整精度（W4A8/W4A16）和存储层级（GPU/内存/SSD），在NVIDIA T4上实现比llama.cpp Q4_0高1.5-1.8倍的解码吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型部署到异构硬件时，瓶颈通常在于内存而非计算能力。现有方法缺乏轻量级的逐层重要性信号来指导精度和内存放置的动态决策。

Method: 提出MCAP（蒙特卡洛激活分析），一种加载时的逐层重要性估计器，生成轻量级信号驱动精度调度（W4A8 vs. W4A16）和驻留层级决策（GPU、RAM、SSD），并构建NVE系统实现统一部署。

Result: 在NVIDIA T4上，NVE系统的解码吞吐量比llama.cpp Q4_0高1.5-1.8倍，并能将模型部署到之前因内存限制而无法运行的场景中，且无需修改权重。

Conclusion: MCAP和NVE通过轻量级逐层分析实现了异构硬件上基于内存预算的动态精度和存储管理，显著提升了解码效率并扩展了部署可行性。

Abstract: Deploying large language models to heterogeneous hardware is often constrained by memory, not compute. We introduce MCAP (Monte Carlo Activation Profiling), a load-time per-layer importance estimator that enables dynamic precision and memory placement decisions on the target device. MCAP produces a lightweight per-layer signal that drives both precision dispatch (W4A8 vs. W4A16) and residency tier (GPU, RAM, SSD), allowing a single set of weights to operate across diverse memory budgets. Our system, NVE, achieves 1.5-1.8x higher decode throughput than llama.cpp Q4_0 on NVIDIA T4 and enables models to run in memory regimes previously infeasible without modifying weights.

</details>


### [115] [Interpretable Quantile Regression by Optimal Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21042)
*Valentin Lemaire,Gaël Aglin,Siegfried Nijssen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种学习最优分位数回归树集合的新方法，能够在保证算法效率的同时，提供可解释的、无需先验假设的条件分布预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习领域对模型的准确性、可解释性和鲁棒性需求日益增长，现有方法难以同时兼顾这些目标。

Method: 提出一种学习最优分位数回归树集合的新方法，在保持单棵树学习效率的同时，学习多棵最优分位数回归树。

Result: 该方法能够：（1）无需先验分布假设即可预测目标变量的完整条件分布；（2）提供可解释的预测；（3）在算法效率上与学习单棵树相当。

Conclusion: 该方法在准确性、可解释性和效率之间取得了良好的平衡，有助于提升用户对AI系统的理解和信任。

Abstract: The field of machine learning is subject to an increasing interest in models that are not only accurate but also interpretable and robust, thus allowing their end users to understand and trust AI systems. This paper presents a novel method for learning a set of optimal quantile regression trees. The advantages of this method are that (1) it provides predictions about the complete conditional distribution of a target variable without prior assumptions on this distribution; (2) it provides predictions that are interpretable; (3) it learns a set of optimal quantile regression trees without compromising algorithmic efficiency compared to learning a single tree.

</details>


### [116] [JEPAMatch: Geometric Representation Shaping for Semi-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21046)
*Ali Aghababaei-Harandi,Aude Sportisse,Massih-Reza Amini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合FlexMatch半监督损失与LeJEPA潜空间正则化项的新方法，通过显式塑造几何表征（鼓励各向同性高斯结构）来解决FixMatch类方法中主导类别偏差和早期伪标签噪声问题，在CIFAR-100、STL-10和Tiny-ImageNet上优于现有基线，并显著加速收敛、降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FixMatch类半监督方法存在两大瓶颈：多数类主导学习过程（被错误伪标签放大）导致模型偏差，以及早期噪声伪标签阻碍清晰决策边界形成、需要漫长训练才能学到有效表征。

Method: 将FlexMatch的半监督损失与LeJEPA衍生的潜空间正则化项（鼓励表征呈各向同性高斯结构）相结合，在传统逻辑输出阈值基础上引入显式几何表征塑造。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、STL-10和Tiny-ImageNet上持续超越现有基线，同时显著加速收敛，大幅降低相比标准FixMatch管线的总体计算成本。

Conclusion: 所提方法在保留伪标签策略优势的同时鼓励结构化表征，为半监督学习从阈值基到几何基的范式转变提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Semi-supervised learning has emerged as a powerful paradigm for leveraging large amounts of unlabeled data to improve the performance of machine learning models when labeled data are scarce.
